# state_pins.txt (N1): words the d3d9 proxy sets when the race phase first becomes 3 (loading starts), in the control AND in ours (va + sh_delta).

# feed the race later (so a mask would hide real state).  Pinning starts every run from the same values; the evolution after the pin is checked.
